migration at Phase 4 SaaS-deploy)\n20\toptions_considered:\n21\t  - decision_a_storage:\n22\t      A1_sqlite_extension: chosen — match BC-6 D-027(a) + Phase 1+2 substrate per L-S17-1 portability; TimescaleDB-style hypertable behavior emulated via (ticker, captured_at) composite index for Phase 3\n23\t      A2_postgres_now_with_timescale: rejected — premature; spec § B.5 declares Postgres+TimescaleDB target but Phase 3 substrate continuity dominates; migration boundary = Phase 4 SaaS-deploy\n24\t      A3_hybrid_sqlite_aggregates_postgres_snapshots: rejected — substrate split adds drift surface without Phase 3 benefit; mirror D-027 A3 rejection rationale\n25\t  - decision_b_adapter_pattern:\n26\t      B1_shared_protocol_three_concretes: chosen — PostAggregator Protocol + 3 concrete classes (F319 + FacebookPublicGroup + ArticleComments-CafeF/Vietstock); shared RateLimitedFetcher base mirroring D-027(b); Liskov-clean swapping for tests\n27\t      B2_three_unrelated_concrete_classes: rejected — duplicates rate-limit + ToS pre-flight + retry logic across 3 platforms\n28\t      B3_single_god_aggregator_with_platform_dispatch: rejected — anti-pattern god service per architecture.md § Forbidden Patterns\n29\t  - decision_c_domain_persistence:\n30\t      C1_domain_layer_aggregate_repository_protocol: chosen — SentimentSnapshot + Narrative + PhaseTransition + PumpDetection + CounterNarrative + LabeledPump aggregates in packages/domain/crowd/; frozen dataclasses + __post_init__ invariants per spec § B.1; Protocol repos in application/crowd/ports/; concrete in infrastructure/crowd/\n31\t      C2_service_layer_only_anemic: rejected — anemic-domain anti-pattern violates architecture.md § Domain Model Rules; spec 003 § B.1 explicitly defines rich entities with bullish_ratio() + dominant_sentiment() + days_in_current_phase() behaviors\n32\t  - decision_d_llm_substrate_boundary:\n33\t      D1_cite_d026_substrate_boundary_verbatim: chosen — per-role override + prose-tolerant JSON + gatherer-wired compute mandatory per D-026 ratified; LLM ONLY for (1) categorical sentiment classification per BR-4 enum 5-level (2) counter-narrative bear-point text per UC-4 (3) pump evidence summary text per UC-3; LLM NEVER outputs numeric per I-S1\n34\t      D2_freeform_llm_no_substrate_pattern: rejected — D-026 binding for ALL Phase 3 LLM-perspective adapters; would violate ratified constitution\n35\t      D3_llm_outputs_numeric_sentiment_score: rejected — violates I-S1 NO-LLM-math hard rule + BR-4 explicit categorical-only mandate\n36\t  - decision_e_narrative_phase_classifier:\n37\t      E1_deterministic_rule_based_seven_state: chosen — 7-phase machine (INCUBATION → EMERGING → MAINSTREAM → SATURATION → EXHAUSTION → REVERSAL → DEAD) per spec § B.3 lines 301-333; thresholds tunable via config; pure-Python no LLM; reproducible classifications per § B.8 bootstrap test\n38\t      E2_llm_classifies_phase_directly: rejected — not reproducible, not calibratable, violates I-S1 (phase = ordinal but classifier output drives event publication = numeric semantic in finance grade); spec § C.1 explicitly rejects this alternative\n39\t      E3_ml_model_trained_on_labeled_phases: rejected — labeled dataset insufficient Phase 3 (5 narratives target per § B.7); deferred to Year 2 outer-loop activation per spec 005\n40\t  - decision_f_pump_phase_classifier:\n41\t      F1_deterministic_multi_signal_weighted_scoring: chosen — 5-state (PRE_PUMP/PUMP/DISTRIBUTION/DUMP/UNCERTAIN) per spec § B.3 lines 379-423; weighted scoring across signals; confidence gate ≥0.4 or returns UNCERTAIN; weights tunable per Karpathy outer loop (Year 2 activation per spec 005)\n42\t      F2_llm_declares_phase_with_confidence: rejected — same rationale as E2; spec § C.1 explicitly rejects \"Let LLM declare pump phase directly\"\n43\t      F3_no_confidence_gate_emit_all_classifications: rejected — would violate § B.10 R6 \"Alerts too noisy, user ignores\" mitigation requiring strict thresholds + historical precision requirement\n44\t  - decision_g_counter_narrative_generator:\n45\t      G1_llm_bear_points_grounded_in_three_sources: chosen — UC-4 verbatim per spec § B.3; LLM bear points grounded in (1) HistoricalAnalogFinder failing analogs (subsequent_return_12m < -0.10) (2) bullish_consensus_summary (3) sector_structural_risks; MANDATORY per BR-6 when sentiment >80% bullish on considered ticker (gates positive signal issuance per I-S13)\n46\t      G2_template_based_no_llm: rejected — spec § C.1 rejects this; counter-narrative quality requires Vietnamese-language LLM synthesis; templated bear-points stale + low-quality\n47\t      G3_llm_freeform_no_grounding: rejected — violates BR-6 + I-S2 provenance + spec § B.10 R3 \"Vietnamese sentiment accuracy <target\" mitigation requiring grounded inputs\n48\t  - decision_h_coordination_detection:\n49\t      H1_feature_extractor_three_signals_high_threshold: chosen — TEMPLATE_SIMILARITY (embedding-based cosine ≥0.9 within 1h window) + TIMING_CLUSTER (>5 accounts within 3-min window repeatedly) + ACCOUNT_AGE_DISTRIBUTION (>50% bullish posters <30-day-old); coordination_score in [0,1]; conservative threshold ≥0.8 per BR-8; **flagged ≠ confirmed** per BR-10 NEVER names operators; framed as \"pattern detected, exercise caution\"\n50\t      H2_llm_judges_coordination: rejected — violates I-S1 (coordination_score is numeric output) + BR-8 reproducibility requirement\n51\t      H3_two_signal_no_account_age: rejected — spec § B.4 explicitly requires 3-feature ensemble; account-age is highest-signal feature for VN F319+FB sock-puppet patterns\n52\t  - decision_i_backtest_validation_gate:\n53\t      I1_precision_recall_holdout_pre_deploy: chosen — precision >0.5 + recall >0.3 on hold-out per BR-5 BEFORE pump classifier deploys live; deferred outcome calibration via labeled_pumps table per spec § B.5; human-review-first for Phase 1 detections per § B.8 (every pump detection in Phase 1 reviewed by user before alert fires)\n54\t      I2_no_validation_gate_ship_then_calibrate: rejected — violates BR-5 explicit mandate + § B.10 R6 \"Alerts too noisy\" mitigation; ship-then-calibrate pattern violates calibration-over-confidence Charter Principle\n55\t      I3_higher_threshold_precision_0_8: rejected — 0.8 precision target unachievable Phase 3 with 5-20 labeled pumps; defer threshold-tightening to Year 2 outer-loop activation per spec 005\n56\tchosen_options: A1 + B1 + C1 + D1 + E1 + F1 + G1 + H1 + I1\n57\t---\n58\t\n59\t# D-032 — BC-7 Crowd Sentiment + Pump Detection Architecture\n60\t"}]},"uuid":"92637ad1-79d0-41e3-bceb-98b207cad1d4","timestamp":"2026-05-06T23:07:45.602Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\decisions\\032-S51-BC-7-architecture-crowd-sentiment.md","content":"---\nid: D-032\ntitle: BC-7 Crowd Sentiment + Pump Detection architecture (Tracks J+K)\nstatus: ACCEPTED\ntier: ARCH\ndate_proposed: 2026-05-06\ndate_ratified: 2026-05-06\nratifying_session: S65\nauthoring_agent: Claude Opus 4.7 (sandwich-architect dispatch; Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Initial Rules (from charter design)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-04-23: LLM Never Outputs Numbers\n25\t**Context**: Stock domain risk — LLM-generated numbers in finance lead to real money loss\n26\t**Rule**: LLM never returns numerical values as natural-language output. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n27\t**Anti-example**:\n28\t```\n29\tLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n30\t```\n31\t**Correct example**:\n32\t```\n33\tLLM tool calls:\n34\t  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n35\t  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n36\t  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\n37\tLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n38\t```\n39\t**Severity**: critical\n40\t**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n41\t\n42\t### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n43\t**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n44\t**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n45\t**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n46\t**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n47\t**Severity**: high\n48\t**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n49\t\n50\t### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data\n51\t**Context**: LLM \"confidence\" is qualitative; user reads numerical values\n52\t**Rule**: Any \"X% confidence\" or \"high/medium/low confidence\" claim must trace to historical hit rate from calibration database. Include `n_samples`, `lookback_period`, `hit_rate` metadata.\n53\t**Anti-example**: \"70% confidence this is a buy\"\n54\t**Correct example**: \"Confidence: MEDIUM (signal pattern: cyclical_bottom_with_dividend; historical n=23 cases since 2018; hit rate 65% over 12 months; lookback=78 months)\"\n55\t**Severity**: high\n56\t**Auto-detect**: partial — flag confidence claims without metadata\n57\t\n58\t### 2026-04-23: Source Provider Disagreement Surfaced\n59\t**Context**: vnstock vs FiinPro vs TCBS sometimes disagree on same data point\n60\t**Rule**: When sources disagree by >1%, output shows divergence explicitly. Never silently pick one.\n61\t**Anti-example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ\" (silent average or arbitrary pick)\n62\t**Correct example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ ± 200 tỷ (vnstock: 35,000 / FiinPro: 35,200 / TCBS: 34,800; reconciled with HIGH confidence)\"\n63\t**Severity**: medium\n64\t**Auto-detect**: no (requires source metadata in output template)\n65\t\n66\t---\n67\t\n68\t## Add New Rules Here\n69\t\n70\t### 2026-04-29: Q&A Bundle Skeleton MUST Carry `defer_cycle` Field\n71\t**Context**: While drafting `qa-escalation/references/sample-bundle.md` in S2, I initially omitted `defer_cycle` from the bundle frontmatter — caught on review against Decision 002 § Track 2 R7 mitigation.\n72\t**Rule**: Every Q&A bundle file in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/` MUST include `defer_cycle: 0` at creation, and increment on every reopening / supersession. SessionStart hook (Track 5) keys can-kicking detection off this field.\n73\t**Anti-example**: Bundle with frontmatter `id / topic / opened_at / expected_answer_by / priority / status` — passes superficial validation but R7 mitigation is invisible.\n74\t**Correct example**: Bundle frontmatter includes `defer_cycle: 0` from creation; every Decision in `related_decisions:` also has `defer_cycles` field syncronized.\n75\t**Severity**: medium\n76\t**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5 `qa-pending-stale-mover.sh` hook should reject bundles missing the field.\n77\t\n78\t### 2026-04-29: User Communication Idiom — \"ok rồi. continue\" / \"ok continue\" = Trivial Acknowledgment\n79\t**Context**: User in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_03.txt` and via chat replies repeatedly used the idiom \"ok rồi. continue\" or \"ok continue\" as the standard \"I accept the proposed defaults; proceed\" signal in Q&A round acceptance flows.\n80\t**Rule**: Treat both phrases as TRIVIAL acknowledgment in lite-detect (`user-prompt-intake/references/lite-detect-patterns.md`). Do NOT dispatch intent-classifier on these. They specifically mean \"accept all defaults of the most recent agent-proposed bundle / decision\"."}]},"uuid":"ac03bc50-f40d-4bf5-aaed-75a44ca97d63","timestamp":"2026-05-06T23:09:19.297Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"# Agent Notes — Learned Rules\n\n> Append-mostly. Full firing-test suite regression S79: 63/63 PASS (61 + 2 new; zero regression). autonomous_mode currently true (re-enabled S48); user-gate before T8 charter ratify. **L-S49b-4 backlog DRAINED at S79** — all 15 non-HH-* wired hooks now have companion firing-tests.\n---\n\n# Phase 3.5 — Harness Deepening (Empirical-Firing Discipline)\n\n## Why this phase exists\n\n**Phase 2.5 verdict (8/8 GREEN, S49a audit)** was a ritual-completion verdict, not an empirical-firing verdict. Audit checked \"hook script exists + wired in settings.json + smoke command exits 0\" — it did NOT check \"hook actually fires on the events it claims to gate\". M-S49b-1 surfaced this gap: `autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh` is wired in Stop chain + smoke-test passes when invoked manually, BUT logs show 0 `Stop session=` entries across ~10 turns of session 5b96635e. Production telemetry contradicts the ritual verdict.\n\n**Three empirical failures surfaced ONLY via user push (post-audit-close)**:\n1. **M-S49b-1**: Stop hook 0 fires for current session despite being wired. Manual invocation works. Suspected Claude Code 4.x Windows quirk under unknown conditions.\n2. **Promote-rule backlog**: Last run S43c (2026-05-04). Now S49b. ~6+ sessions accumulated agent-notes entries without promotion cycle running. `promotion-cycle-trigger.sh` exists but did not fire (likely Stop-hook-dependent — see #1).\n3. **Auto-detect candidates without companion shipped hook**: ~20 entries in agent-notes tagged `Auto-detect: yes` that never got promoted to actual deterministic hook. Visible only via manual grep, not via any harness alarm.\n\n**Anti-pattern codified**: \"ritual completion without empirical-firing verification\". Counter-rule (T8 ratification target): **\"Harness must self-verify firing, not self-attest existence\"**.\n\n**Confidence assessment**: Phase 2.5 \"post-audit\" autonomous-readiness was claimed ~7/10. Real production behavior is ~5/10 (defects surfaced via user, not self-detected). After Phase 3.5 close: target ~8.5/10 with empirical evidence backing every claim.\n\n---\n\n## Hard rules during Phase 3.5\n\n1. **Each track = separate session** per CLAUDE.md never-mix-PLAN-with-IMPL doctrine. Multi-track-IMPL allowed only when tracks are mechanically independent (T2 + T6 are; T3 needs T2 done first).\n2. **Every hook authored in this phase MUST ship with a companion firing-test** (T7 retrofit codifies this rule). Firing-test = bash script that triggers the hook's gate event in a controlled fashion + asserts the hook produced the expected log/file artifact. Unit-test of script logic is NOT enough — empirical fire required.\n3. **No silent re-attestation**. If a track marks \"DONE\" but T6 harness-health-self-scan signals FAIL on its self-check, the track is NOT closed; root-cause first.\n4. **VBW pre-flight Glob-before-Read** on every target file edit (L-S30-1 doctrine).\n5. **Edit not Write for protected paths** (L-S45-2 + write-vs-edit-guard.sh active).\n6. **Per L-S49b-3**: every Agent.dispatch() MUST be preceded by pre-dispatch in-flight check (grep observations + grep dispatch-pending JSONL); dispatch FIRST then write checkpoint with `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` field; end turn after checkpoint write per L-S49b-4 carve-out.\n7. **No business-logic IMPL on Phase 3 Track J/K/L during Phase 3.5** — focus is harness, not BC-7/BC-9.\n8. **Charter ratification (T4 + T8) requires AskUserQuestion gate** — neither track may self-execute the charter edit. Ratify via deny-lift mechanism (S38 precedent) or AskUserQuestion explicit \"yes promote\".\n\n---\n\n## Track summary matrix (8 tracks, T1..T8)\n\n| Track | Session | Type | Effort (main) | Effort (subagent) | Dependency | Output artifact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **T1** Phase 2.5 post-mortem | S51 | POST-MORTEM | 30-50K | — | none (entry track) | `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/2026-05-XX-phase-2.5-empirical-firing-gap.md` |\n| **T2** Stop→UserPromptSubmit migration | S52 | FOCUSED_IMPL | 20-40K | — | T1 done | settings.json edit + N hooks remapped + smoke firing-test |\n| **T3** Promote-rule cycle backlog dispatch | S53 | MULTI_TASK_IMPL + sandwich | 80-120K | 80-120K | T2 done (pre-dispatch check needs reliable firing) | `agent-workspace/memory/observations/promote-rule-S53.md` + N new hook scripts |\n| **T4** Charter promote L-S49b-4 + 3 checkpoint hooks | S54 | FOCUSED_IMPL + AskUserQuestion gate | 30-50K | — | T1 done;
